ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u7	Thank you, sir. We proceed with the examination of items 1st of the agenda: Report from the Committee on Justice and Human Rights on the Government's draft law on bailiffs (Prints No 1582 and 2032). I ask Mr Waldemar Buda to submit a report to the committee.
ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u8	Mr. Marshal! Minister! Prime Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights, I have the honour to present a report on the government draft law on bailiffs, print No 1582. Let me remind you that this is a new law that regulates the status of a bailiff again, introduces a number of new solutions that are moving in this direction, so that, first of all, it will increase supervision of the activities of the bailiffs, secondly, lead to the situation of personal bailiffs, and thirdly, increase efficiency in their operation. A number of changes proposed by the Act are accepted by the bailiffs' environment. Of course, there are conflict points, there are points of doubt and discussion, but most of them we managed to discuss and make changes that satisfy the whole bailout environment. Let me draw your attention to the basic assumptions. First of all, we limit the number of cases that can be handled by one law firm to a maximum of 5,000. This is very important because it will lead to an even distribution of all cases throughout Poland, in all chamber offices. Secondly, we limit the chamber section to the area of appeal, which is most often to the borders of the voivodship. And thirdly, a very important solution is filming the activities of the bailiff in the field. It is a request of both creditors and bailiffs to document the activities so that there is no doubt - in the course of the activities carried out - whether there have been lawful acts or activities that have exceeded those limits. A number of amendments were made to the committee during its work. Most of these were legislative amendments aimed at clarifying the provisions proposed in the original draft, but there were also quite significant amendments that were going precisely towards concessions for the bailout environment. I would like to draw attention here, for example, to the higher powers of the asessors who, according to the original version of the project, were not able in principle to carry out activities in the field. The draft, after the work of the committee, sets out a number of exemptions in this respect and a number of activities which the experts will nevertheless be able to carry out on their own. There are also a number of changes that aim to bring about effective disciplinary action, because, of course, there were a lot of cases in which this procedure did not work. The supervision of the Minister of Justice today will indeed be realistic and will give the Minister the right to react immediately if irregularities are detected. So far it has been very different, it has been difficult to suspend the bailiff, it has been difficult to remove him from his duties even with bright and obvious irregularities. Therefore, on behalf of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights, I present the proposal and recommend further work on this project. Thank you very much. ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u9	Thank you, sir. ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u10	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Law and Justice Club, I present my position on the government draft law on bailiffs, prints No 1582 and 2032. It is a new law governing the profession of bailiff, repealing the previous, 1997, on bailiffs and executions. This law lays down the rules of service and the rights and obligations of bailiffs, the rules of recruitment and preparation for the exercise of the profession, the rules of operation of the bailiff's self-government and the matters of disciplinary responsibility, and the rules of supervision of bailiffs and bailiffs. As regards administrative supervision, the principle remains that the bailiff remains a public officer operating at a specific district court. The President of the District Court has been provided with additional supervisory powers and the judicate supervision is exercised by the court. In carrying out enforcement and protective activities, bailiffs shall exercise public authority and be its body. The bailiff conducts his business as a profession of public trust and performs his activities personally, which is a principle and specific derogations may apply to bailiffs. A statutory definition of the law firm has been introduced. As far as the local jurisdiction of the bailiffs is concerned, the bailiff is to accept cases only from the area of appeal - that is one or two voivodeships - and it is limited to accepting cases from the choice of the creditor to 5 thousand. a year, which is intended to reduce the so-called phenomenon. Chamber wholesalers. Specific requirements have been laid down for the position of bailiff. First of all, it is Polish citizenship, full capacity for legal activities, impeccable character, uncriminality, completed higher law studies, held bailiff's application and passed chamber examination, at least 2 years of work as bailiff's assistant and ending 28 years at the time of appointment - I will remind you that earlier was the requirement of 26 years. The bailiff is appointed by the Minister of Justice. The Minister of Justice may refuse to appoint a bailiff when the average of cases in a given district would be less than 1 000. cases per bailiff in a given calendar year, excluding cases from the choice of the creditor. The bailiff is bound by court decisions which can directly interfere with enforcement activities. The Minister of Justice may suspend the bailiff from his duties. The Minister's order is an appeal. The Minister may withdraw the bailiff from office in very specific cases: if he considers him completely unfit for the duties of bailiff, in the event that the bailiff causes a shortage of more than 15 000. zł, in the absence of a civil liability agreement, in the case of the age of 65, except that there are transitional provisions, which provide that if the bailiff has completed 65 years before the entry into force of the legal act, he may work until its entry into force. The appointment as bailiff expires by law, if the bailiff died, resigned from the duties of bailiff, was legally punished with disciplinary punishment of expulsion from the bailiff service, was legally convicted of a intentional or deliberate tax offence, lost Polish citizenship, was legally incapacitated wholly or partially. In a specific chapter, the rights and obligations of the bailiff are defined. The bailiff, among others. he is not an entrepreneur, he may not take on additional employment, except in the scientific, teaching or teaching post of a higher education institution, provided that the performance of such employment does not interfere with the performance of the duties of bailiff. As regards the liability of the bailiff, he is obliged to remedy the damage caused by unlawful acts or omissions in the performance of his duties. The Treasury shall be liable for the damage in solidarity with the bailiff. It shall constitute an Article. Thirty-six percent of the bill. The State Treasury shall, in the event of compensation, have a return claim to the bailiff, unless the damage has been caused solely by the bailiff's compliance with the court's orders or administrative supervisory authorities. The appointment of a deputy bailiff has also been defined where he cannot carry out his duties due to legal or factual obstacles. Then the chairman of the competent district court shall appoint an alternate bailiff by order, which may be another bailiff or an assistant employed in the law firm of the absent bailiff, but this assistant must meet the requirement of at least two years' work. In a separate chapter, the issue of liquidation of the chamber firm was also addressed in detail, as was the issue of the bailiff's application, the principle of the initial examination for the application, the issue of the appointment of the selection board and its composition, the conditions for the application and the preparation of questions by the panel appointed by the Minister of Justice after consulting the National Chamber Council by means of a minister's regulation. This team consists of four representatives of the Minister of Justice, two representatives of the National Chamber Council and one scientific, scientific and educational or teaching staff. In detail, the rules in the area of bail-in applications, bail-out exams and the rights and duties of the assessors have also been regulated. The Presidents of the competent court of appeal shall appoint bailiffs. The bailiff is obliged to employ at least one assessor over a period of 3 years. The statutory standards also regulate the costs of the bailiff's enforcement activities and the rules for running the bailiff's law firm. The supervision of bailiffs is exercised by the Minister of Justice, the Presidents of Appeal Courts and the Presidents of District Courts, while the internal supervision is exercised by the bailiff's local government. Inspections and visits of chamber firms are provided. The bailiffs shall be required to submit annual reports on their activities to the administrative and local authorities. The organs of the bailiff's self-government are the National Chambers, the National Audit Committee and the Chambers. Chapter 11 contains provisions on disciplinary liability. Disciplinary penalties are the same as for other legal authorities, i.e. warning, reprimand, fines, suspension of activities, removal from service or deprivation of the right to pursue a profession, but there is a new form of disciplinary penalty in the bailiffs' law, i.e. the penalty for prohibiting the admission of cases from the choice of creditor for a period of between 1 and 3 years. The draft law amends a number of trade union laws, mostly in the enforcement rules of the Code of Civil Procedure. These changes in these provisions are 38. They aim to simplify these procedures. The amendments also extend the powers of enforcement authorities to request clarification from public authorities. A complaint against the bailiff's activities and an enforcement request may be lodged on an official form. There is also a record concerning recording by means of an image recording device and sound in the scope of execution operations carried out by the bailiff outside the bailiff's office. This action is 12. They are listed in Article. 809 c.p.c. The limitation of enforcement in Article has also been extended. 829 c.p.c. for such household appliances, which so far has not been, such as fridge, washing machine, oven, microwave oven, hob, beds, table and chairs in the number necessary. In the interim rules, the principle is that the existing provisions apply to cases initiated and not completed on the date of entry into force of the Act. On behalf of the club, I'm making three more amendments. These amendments concern the clarification of the amendments to the Article. 801 k.p.c., Art. 801 Civil Procedure Code. [[And the cost?]] They also deal with matters relating to disciplinary responsibility as regards applicants and bailiffs. In addition, the date of entry into force of this Law shall be postponed to 1 January 2019, except for Article. Article 12 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 and Article 1 288. These articles concern administrative proceedings before the Minister of Justice for the appointment of a bailiff. The extension of the date of entry into force of this law is due to the registration of these activities of the bailiff outside the law firm. On behalf of the club, I request the endorsement of this bill. I'm placing three amendments on the hands of the Marshal. [[♪ The bell ♪]] Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u11	Thank you very much. Mr Wojciech Wilk, Civic Platform.
ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u12	Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Civic Platform Parliamentary Club, I would like to present my position on the report of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights on the Government draft law on bailiffs, print No 2032. For the express pace of work in the Committee on Justice and Human Rights concerning government draft laws in the field of the Ministry of Justice, it must be said that the work on this bill lasted quite long, since the first reading took place in June. Several dozen substantive amendments have been made in the course of the project in both the subcommittee and the Committee on Justice and Human Rights, and more than 1,000 have been tabled. legislative comments, so it cannot be said that the draft submitted in the Sejm was a well-prepared project. New amendments have been tabled today, which confirms my thesis. At first reading, speaking on behalf of the club, I said that the justification for the bill does not really specify what the overriding purpose of this law is. I also drew attention to some of the negative consequences of the project that we are talking about for bailiffs, but what is also important - for creditors. It is appropriate to agree with the opinions that the proposed provisions are often characterised by a lack of justification, coherence or precision. The project also often duplicates the errors of the applicable regulations and introduces solutions which not only serve to improve the functioning of the judicial enforcement system, but, on the contrary, announce a deterioration in the conditions of the bailiff's profession and the dysfunction of the judicial enforcement system, which contradicts the declared objectives of the new regulation. Some of these doubts have been clarified during the committee's work, as amendments have been tabled, but some comments and questions remain valid, for example. The interference of the legislator in constitutionally defined laws of the bailiff's self-government as a self-government profession of public trust raises doubts. It must be said that the project significantly increases the supervisory and control powers of the Minister of Justice over bailiffs. In the event of gross or persistent infringement by the bailiff, he will be able to revoke the bailiff from office. Currently, it is not possible for the Minister of Justice to initiate proceedings for the dismissal of the bailiff. The Minister will also be able to suspend the bailiff at any stage of disciplinary proceedings. In addition, the President of the District Court will be able to withdraw immediately from the bailiff's activities, which grossly infringes the law, and directly request the Minister of Justice to withdraw him from office. The frequency of bailiffs' checks by the Presidents of the courts will be increased. At present, such a check must be carried out at least once every four years, and this will be done once every two years after the amendment. The ad hoc checks may be carried out at any time. The court will ex officio examine the correctness of the bailiff's activities. If errors are detected in the bailiff's activities, the court will have to correct the error. In turn, all enforcement activities in the field will be recorded and the recorded image and sound will be part of the case file. The question is whether such recordings will violate the right to privacy and the standards of protection of the rights of the debtor, the creditor as well as third parties. According to some opinions, such a solution will also create additional costs, which may be burdensome especially for small firms. Comments should be made on the provisions on administrative supervision of the bailiff. In the opinion of Prof. Andrzej Marciniak, the draft law duplicates the previous legislative error granting the Minister of Justice the competence to appoint a disciplinary officer and his alternates. According to the author of this opinion, this is a limitation of the powers of professional self-government in favour of the representative of the executive authority. The Commission's report on Justice and Human Rights provides for the restoration of public enforcement fees and therefore will constitute a full charge for the Treasury, and the bailiff, depending on the results of his work, will be entitled to a commission remuneration fixed in proportion to the amount of the fees collected. Such a solution raises concern about the financial collapse of many law firms, which can lead to a situation in which, instead of addressing courts and bailiffs, entrepreneurs will use all kinds of debt collection companies, and this can lead to a situation in which the bailiffs will take over the place of the companies, which unfortunately happens, operating on the border of law. Prof. Mariusz Jabłoński pointed out in his opinion to the bill that the institutions of the deputy bailiff and the curator of the law firm established in the draft law were not sufficiently precisely regulated. In turn, the procedure for their designation is characterised by discretion and arbitrariness, deepened by the absence of any appeal procedures. Also, the legal status of the bailiff developed by this draft law contains some ambiguities, the formation of the rights and obligations of the bailiff was based on a combination of characteristics characteristic for different legal relations. The employment ratio of the bailiff on the basis of the bill combines the characteristics of the independent activity, the employment relationship and the indeterminate business relationship. According to dr Ewa Staszewska leaving the project in such a state constitutes a kind of privatisation of public authority. In turn, according to Prof. Andrzej Marciniak, the proposal for a change concerning the legal status of the bailiff threatens to reduce the effectiveness of the judicial execution to a level prior to the entry into force of the 1997 law on bailiffs and executions. There are also doubts about the proposed provisions concerning the bail-in application, which do not provide for the possibility for applicants to carry out certain activities themselves, while the application is intended to prepare the profession in practice. In my opinion, the inability to entrust bailiff applicants with certain activities on behalf of the bailiff may result in the applicants not being properly prepared for the occupation. The possibility of entrusting only office and order activities to the applicant after one year of application is insufficient in the proper training process. It is stressed that all laws governing the functioning of legal professions provide for the possibility for applicants to perform certain activities themselves. It is enough to indicate the provisions of the Law on Lawyers or Law on Lawyers, which provide for the possibility of the applicant being replaced by a patron before common courts. Disabling bailiffs to act on their own during the application may result in the execution of the person who completes the application only after having been appointed as bailiff. The proposed education system will have a negative impact on its quality. The bailiffs will also be able to carry out enforcement activities only on an exceptional basis. So the bailiffs will have to do their job personally and alone. It also appears that the introduction of such restrictions will significantly impede the learning of applicants and the reliable preparation of an asessor to carry out execution tasks on his own in the future and will make it difficult to carry out enforcement procedures efficiently and effectively. According to Dr. Grzegorz Julke, the act presented contrary to the announcements does not constitute a new act which would properly and modernly regulate the functioning of judicial enforcement bodies. The law does not contain new solutions, it is mostly a duplication of existing legislation, it only significantly extends the system of supervision of bailiffs and significantly restricts the functioning of the bailiff's authorities. There is also serious concern about the model of financing executions, which is a significant reduction in funds for bailiffs to carry out execution activities, which may, according to many experts, lead to the collapse of executions in Poland. To conclude, Mr Marshal, Minister, High Izbo, I would like to say that the final decision on whether to support or vote against this law will be taken after an examination of the amendments tabled today. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

I would like to remind you that out of each 1000 PLN in this situation 100 PLN will have to be allocated for bail-out fees. Count it to the working hours of an employee in Poland. These are unacceptable things, because what are they causing as a result, ladies and gentlemen? If the debtor does not repay, the creditor will not receive the money. I guess the role of execution and the role of bailiff is that the creditor gets money from the debtor. ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u18	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Poselski Club I present a modern position on the report of the Parliamentary Committee on Justice and Human Rights on the draft law on bailiffs, print No 2032. The declared aim of the project is to introduce solutions to restore confidence in bailiffs as public officers and to increase supervision of bailiffs, and to create consistent regulation in the above matters. Unfortunately, in all these aspects, failure is to be expected if solutions proposed by the Ministry of Justice are introduced. The project submitted is insufficient and unclear to determine the status of the bailiff. Although the proposed solutions indicate that the bailiff remains a public officer who benefits from special criminal protection and is subject to the rules of criminal law applicable to public officials, the principle of exclusive jurisdiction of judicial bailiffs as the only one in addition to the courts of enforcement bodies carrying out judgments of the courts of the Republic of Poland is also maintained, however, the following provisions indicate that as a public officer it remains in a specific relationship with the State Treasury and - I quote from the justification of the project - receives commission remuneration, deducted from enforcement fees, which are of a public nature. On the other hand, in the same law, the rules indicate the abnormal working time of the bailiff, determined solely by the number of tasks, the business formula, i.e. the independent individual law firm, as well as the commission remuneration, which unfortunately is to be taxed on goods and services, which is the result of the law. The efficiency of the judicial system consists not only of the speed of court proceedings and the legitimacy of judgments taken by courts, but also of the effectiveness of enforcement of those judgments. The provisions of this law are in contrast to the principle of the correctness of judicial proceedings. According to the National Chamber Council, the proposed solutions can contribute to the liquidation of up to 60% of bail-out firms. Last year we had a very important number of quit jobs that did not take place in previous years. In view of the above, a significant decrease in the efficiency of the enforcement of judgments is expected. In this way, unfortunately, instead of helping to enforce maintenance claims or employee claims, the government paradoxically creates a certain protective umbrella for the kind of debtors who prey on citizens. This bill will in no way increase confidence in the profession of bailiff. There are also bad records regarding the supervision of bailiffs. They are inconsistent with the justification of the law, lead to duplication of supervisory activities and once again confirm the excessive competence of the Minister of Justice. Despite the declaration that the President of the regional court is the most important, fundamental link of administrative supervision, this role will actually be played by the Minister of Justice. It is for him that full competence is reserved, starting with the formulation of questions for the application exam, and ending with the possibility of dismissing bailiffs. In conclusion, the proposed project does not meet its basic objectives. He's inconsistent and incomprehensible. There is no doubt that the adoption of this law will not in any way contribute to the elimination of distortions in the judicial enforcement system, but will only lead to chaos and confusion, which will adversely affect the rights of ordinary citizens to pursue their debts. In view of the fact that it is a legal buble, Members of Modern Affairs will vote against this law. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u52	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present our position on the report of the committee on the government draft law on bailiffs on behalf of the club of the Polish People's Party. The proposed law is intended to replace the existing law of 29 August 1997 on bailiffs and executions. It introduces comprehensive legal solutions to improve the standard of action of the bailiff in the area of enforcement. According to the project, supervisory and control powers of the Minister of Justice over bailiffs are to be substantially increased. He will be able, in the course of administrative proceedings, to take action ex officio, even to remove the bailiff from his post and, earlier, to suspend him if the bailiff grossly violates the law. According to the proposed amendments, the real supervision of the governors of the regional courts over the operation of bailiffs is also to be increased. The project gives the governors of the regional courts the power to remove the bailiff from the activities carried out for 30 days and to apply directly to the Minister of Justice for the dismissal of the bailiff. Today, such powers are not available to the presidents of the regional courts. According to the project, they will be obliged to take every action against bailiffs who violate the law or the principles of professional ethics. If the chairman of the district court has allowed a flagrant omission in the supervision of the bailiff, he himself will be exposed to an appeal from his post. Another important change is the introduction of an obligation to record individual enforcement activities of the bailiff carried out in the field. Such recording shall be part of the file of conduct included in the protocol. At the time of execution, the bailiff will be obliged to provide the debtor with a clear form of the complaint so that everyone can refer it to the competent court if there is an irregularity. Today, more than 80% of bailiff complaints are not taken into account by courts, often due to minor formal deficiencies. The project also introduces the principle that when the debtor's apartment is forced to be opened or searched by the bailiff, this must be done in the police's assistance. The rules for carrying out executions from mobility have been amended, among others. by specifying precisely the movables that are not occupied. This is intended to limit the arbitraryness of applicants in decisions that may deprive the debtor of the items necessary for his daily existence. These include items and household appliances such as a refrigerator, washing machine, vacuum cleaner, bed or stove for preparing meals. The list of infringements for which the bailiffs will be liable is to be extended. Such actions will be accelerated. Chambers will be controlled at least once every 2 years, and not once every 4 years, as they are now. Financial control will also be carried out by the head of the competent tax office. According to the new regulations, the bailiff is to act in person and the activities he has assigned to the assessors can only have a training function as a preparation for the profession. The bailiff has all the responsibility, including compensation, for the proper functioning of the assessors. The aim of increasing the professionalism of bailiffs is to require that they have to have a higher education in law. Today 30% of bailiffs have no higher legal education, and about 9% He's barely a middle-school student. They will have to finish their legal studies within 7 years from 1 January of the year following the date of entry into force of the new law, or they will leave the profession. Since the bailiff, as a public servant, must have the right experience in life, the project raises the minimum age limit of a person appointed to this post from 26 years to 28. This solution is similar to the regulation of the lower age limit of the judge. At the same time, the upper limit of the age of the bailiff is reduced from 70 years to 65 years. This is justified by the special circumstances of the bailiff's work in the field. In addition to high legal qualifications, this profession also requires good physical fitness and psychophysical resistance. According to the project, the appointment as bailiff of persons who at the date of entry into force of the Act are 65 years old expires 2 years after the date of entry into force of the Act, but no later than the date of the end of 70 years of age. The bailiff will be able to operate mainly in his territory. It is only the absence of a delay in dealing with cases in the territory of more than 6 months that will give rise to a premium consisting in the possibility of accepting cases from the creditor's choice, subject to the fact that the bailiff will have the right to handle these cases only within the limits of the appeal. In addition, the bailiff will not be able to accept cases by choice of creditor when the impact of all cases in a given year exceeds 2,5 thousand and the effectiveness of his executions in the previous year will not exceed 35%, and when the impact of all cases in a given year exceeds 5 000. The statement of property made by the bailiffs will be supplemented by a clear indication of the income from the activities of the firm and revenue obtained from other sources and their amount. A copy of the statement will be sent to the competent tax office. The declaration shall be made public, following those concerning the Judges. The bailiff will have clear rules on the possibility of additional earnings - only scientific and teaching work - and business restrictions. The bailiff will only be able to live off the salary of the firm's work. These solutions will make the bailiff's status similar to that of a judge or prosecutor and eliminate situations where the bailiff could act in conflict of interest. The new rules change the shape of the bailiff's application - greater emphasis will be placed on the adoption of ethical principles and reliability in the pursuit of the bailiff's profession. It was proposed that the scope of the preliminary examination should include, inter alia,: material criminal law, matters relating to the administration of courts and the pursuit of the profession of bailiff. In the course of the committee's work, amendments to the draft were tabled, inter alia. providing that in the first 2 years of operation the bailiff will not be able to accept more than 2,5 thousand. matters from the choice of creditor per year. High Izbo! We were all appalled by the loud matter of the tractor occupation by the bailiff at Mława. There are many such cases in Poland, and without a doubt the conditions for fulfilling this extremely important social function, which is the function of the bailiff, require changes. However, it is not possible to go indifferently alongside how the proposed changes are addressed by the representatives of the bailiffs themselves. The bailiffs point out that such a far-reaching reduction in the powers of assesors and inevitable staff shortages in law firms will mainly affect single parents who will have problems with the execution of alimony, as well as receiving money from the maintenance fund. Now, in order to receive funds from the Fund, a certificate of unsuccessful execution must be presented. However, the bailiff can only spend it if he fails to obtain any money from the debtor, but not earlier than 2 months after the execution. It also needs to make sure that the debtor does not have any assets, and for this purpose, among others. carry out field activities at his place of residence. Only then can he issue an appropriate certificate. Changes in the bill according to the bailiffs may prolong this process and parents without money from the fund may for a long time be without means of life. You can also ask how applicants and assistants are to learn their profession in the light of such a drastic limitation of their rights in bailiffs. Their position will in fact be limited to the role of office worker, postman or camera operator. The Asessor will be able to go to the debtor, but he will have no right to enforce anything, and the debtor will simply be able to chase him away. In the course of field operations, which will only be carried out by the bailiff, applicants and assessors will only be able to hold the camera, i.e. their practical ability to prepare for the profession will be illusory. It's more or less as if the student was sitting only in the passenger's seat on his driving licence course. The project also has positive aspects. The obligation to register executions should contribute to improving compliance by bailiffs. Never enough publicity in public life. A precise definition of movements that are not occupied, i.e. to satisfy elementary needs, such as: The gas stove, also to a greater extent than today, will secure the interest of the debtor. The obligation to provide the debtor with a clear complaint form, so that everyone can refer it to the competent court, should increase the ability and knowledge of debtors to lodge effective complaints on the work of bailiffs.
ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u53	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Freedom and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the government draft law on bailiffs, prints No. 1582 and 2032. Procededed laws are the answer to the drastic actions of some bailiffs. The most famous cases concerned the occupation of a farmer's tractor from Mława and the occupation of the car owner of one of the companies in Gostynin. They have grown into a symbol of the negative actions of the bailiffs, but in practice we can find much more negative phenomena in this field of life. The need for a new legal act to restore confidence in the profession of bailiff as a public officer and to increase supervision of bailiffs is indisputable. During the intensive work of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights and the extraordinary subcommittee on the government draft law on bailiffs, a number of legislative and substantive comments have been taken into account, which create a very coherent and comprehensive new law. Although the bill is very broad and its material is quite complex, the direction of the changes is consistent with the idea of transparent and effective execution. At the same time, standards are introduced which are accepted by both creditors and debtors. In order to increase the effectiveness of executions, quantitative changes have been made in the execution of executions by one bailiff at the same time. Or one law firm. The tightening act links the efficiency and backlog criteria in the cases under discussion, which means introducing a rule that the better the effectiveness and the less the backlog, the more matters that can be led by choice. In the context of the existence of irregularities in bail-in activities, it is appropriate to increase the supervisory and control powers of the Minister of Justice in respect of bailiffs. This should be served by recording all enforcement activities carried out in the field, while the recorded image and sound will be part of the case file. This change is crucial for checking the correctness of operations as well as impartiality and ethical standards of bailiffs' work. In addition, in the event of a gross or persistent infringement by the bailiff, the Minister of Justice will be able to exercise effectively the power of appeal of the bailiff of his own motion. The Minister will also be able to suspend the bailiff at any stage of disciplinary proceedings. In addition, according to the project, the Presidents of the courts will carry out checks on the work of the bailiffs at least once every 2 years. The bill provides appropriate guarantees to ensure that the bailiff is able to perform his duties properly. In addition, the bill removes many unclear rules that gave room for various interpretations, which led to pathological phenomena within the sphere of chamber activities. An example is the freedom of the assessors to carry out enforcement activities, which gave them the opportunity to take off the responsibilities of the bailiffs themselves on behalf of whom they acted. Currently, the project is moving towards defining all the powers in terms of activities that the assessor himself can undertake, and guaranteeing many of those powers that the assessors have performed today for the sake of personal execution by the bailiff. The profession of bailiff is a profession of public trust, not a business. In carrying out his duties, the bailiff enters directly into the constitutionally protected rights and freedoms of citizens, including fundamental property rights. We are convinced that the pending draft laws, after their adoption and entry into force, will restore the professional bailiff's high prestige. This will be served by increasing the supervision of the Minister of Justice over the activities of the bailiff's authorities, including among others. granting the Minister of Justice the power to verify the correctness of the entries made on the list of bailiffs. Maintaining the transparency of the enforcement process and the rules for the selection of bailiffs will improve the social acceptance of this important sphere of economic and civil trade. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The Free and Solidarni Circle supports the government bill on bailiffs.

ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u57	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Law and Justice Club, I present my position on the Government's draft bill on the costs of bailiffs, seym prints No. 1582 and 2030. The project shall set out the amount of the bail-in costs, the rules for carrying them and the procedure for dealing with those costs. Until now, the bail-in costs have been regulated by the system law on bailiffs and executions of 1997, and I would like to remind you that they were already regulated by the regulations of the Minister of Justice. The development of the new law calls for transparent rules for the financing of the enforcement system and for arrangements to motivate debtors to voluntarily repay the commitments identified by enforcement titles. The bail-in costs consist of the bailiff's expenses and the bail-out fees. The expenditures are among others. experts' and translators' fees, adverts' costs, specialist transport costs, travel costs outside the town, which is the seat of the chamber firm, charges for persons appointed under separate rules to participate in operations, costs of obtaining documents or information necessary for the conduct of proceedings, costs of delivery of correspondence, except for the costs of service to the parties of the notice of initiation of execution, safeguard proceedings or proceedings for the execution of a European order for security in the bank account, costs for the operation of the bailiff outside the chamber, costs for the transfer of funds by post or abroad, costs of payment of the court fee due on the application for entry in the perpetual register. The bailiff shall have the right to advance expenditure. If the bailiff chosen by the creditor undertakes activities outside the bailiff's jurisdiction, expenses in the form of allowances granted to the bailiff and to persons employed in and participating in the bailiff's chambers, the costs of arrival of the bailiff and those persons and the costs of the specialised equipment shall be borne by the creditor even if he is entitled to an exemption from the bailiff's costs. These expenses shall not be included in the costs charged to the debtor. The bail-in fees shall include enforcement fees for the execution of enforcement proceedings, the execution of the security of the claim or the European Account Preservation Order and fees for other proceedings or other actions. The bailiff only collects or collects bail-in fees in the cases specified in the Act. The bail-in fees are either relative or fixed. The enforcement fee for the execution of cash benefits shall be collected by the bailiff together with the enforcement of the benefit in proportion to the value of the enforced benefit. The payment of the fee shall not be subject to a decision. The calculation of the comparative fee shall be based on the value of the benefit. The comparative fees in the case must not be less than PLN 150 and higher than PLN 50 000. zł. Enforcement fees for cash benefit enforcement cases are 10% the value of the enforced benefit. I wanted to stress here the principle of this law, i.e. the motivation of the debtor to pay the debt voluntarily. If, within one month of the date of delivery of the notice of initiation of execution, the debtor pays all or part of the benefits to the bailiff or to his bank account, the bailiff shall charge him a relative fee of 3%. , not 10% that is in favour of the debtor. And where the amount paid to the creditor by the debtor does not constitute an enforceable benefit if the debtor fulfils the benefit within one month in the hands of the bailiff or in his account or directly to the creditor, then what is the charge? The fee is then 150 PLN. If the benefit was enforced only as a result of the enforcement of the claim, bank account, salary for work or social security benefits after the end of the month, there is a minimum fee of PLN 200. Enforcement fees for enforcement of non-monetary benefits are mostly fixed fees, e.g. the application for execution for the issue of movable goods amounts to PLN 400. The fixed fees on the application for possession of immovable property intended to satisfy the debtor's housing needs or to empty the premises or premises intended to satisfy such needs shall be PLN 1500 regardless of the number of dwellings. And so far it was simply from each chamber and often the debtor moved out voluntarily, and the bailiff set such a height. It was sometimes even several thousand zlotys. The total fee in these cases shall not exceed 30 000. zł. Of course, there is also an exemption from commercial costs granted to the parties under the law and granted in exploratory proceedings. This extends to enforcement proceedings. Notwithstanding this, a person who does not benefit from such exemption or is not able to bear the necessary expenses or fees to be paid by the bailiff may apply to the district court for exemption from all or part of those costs. And here the rules on judicial costs in civil matters apply accordingly. This is the law of 28 July 2005. In the event of an exemption, the district court at which the bailiff operates shall pay temporarily to the bailiff, at his request, the sums corresponding to the amount of expenses and the fees on which the bailiff's action depends. Provisions concerning the reduction of the enforcement fee for the execution of cash benefits are also important. The debtor may make such a request if the special circumstances so warrant: a condition relating to the work of the bailiff, but also to the debtor's property situation and the amount of his income. The draft of this law contains six amendments to the Code of Civil Procedure, among others. specifies that the application for entry in the perpetual book submitted by the head of the tax office shall be subject to a fee. This is an exception to the principle that the State Treasury is not charged. There are also amendments to the Law on Judicial Costs in Civil Matters, which are very important, because they specify fixed fees in the complaint for the actions of the bailiff - PLN 50, in the complaint for refusal to perform a notarial act - PLN 100, in the execution by the court of execution of an act which another person cannot perform for the debtor - that is, in accordance with Article. 1050 or 1051 Civil Procedure Code. And there is, of course. also a transitional provision. With regard to proceedings initiated and not completed before the date of entry into force of the Act, the existing provisions shall apply with two exceptions: if the creditor has not carried out the necessary action to continue the proceedings within 6 months or has not requested a suspended procedure. The implementing provisions adopted pursuant to the previous law shall remain in force until the date of entry into force of the implementing provisions pursuant to this Law. But here, High Izbo, I also want to make six amendments on behalf of the Law and Justice Club. They relate to the flat-rate costs of consolidating activities outside the law firm and storing image and sound recording, provided that the creditor requested that the action be maintained. The bailiff, who takes steps outside the office and keeps these activities going, can also charge a fee of PLN 50 regardless of the number of operations, the volume of the data recorded in the act. The next two amendments concern a reduced fee which cannot be less than PLN 200 - that is, of course, at the request of the debtor - and a new date for the entry into force of the Act: as of 1 January 2019. I propose these amendments to the Marshal and request that the High House support the entire draft and these amendments. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u65	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Freedom and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the Government's draft bill on bail-out costs, prints No. 1581 and 2030. The bill is based on the need to develop a new regulation on the operation of bailiffs. For this reason, a separate law includes a range of fees and costs for the operation of bailiffs, which is a good solution because of the need for greater flexibility in shaping the public's burden on fees resulting from the enforcement system. The draft law on commercial costs simplifies the catalogue of enforcement fees and introduces several new solutions, such as a reduction in the enforcement fee in case of voluntary debt repayment. The fundamental objective of the law, the reduction of the bail-in costs on the part of both the debtor and the creditor, is maintained, but with the income of the bailiff at the appropriate level. At the meeting of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights, there were some controversy over the level of bail-in fees. In particular, a live discussion led to the setting of a 10% rate of the fee and a relatively high level of additional fixed charges for small-value loans. However, we must bear in mind the need to maintain the stability of the functioning of public enforcement bodies by ensuring adequate income for bailiffs, while balancing the interests of all entities and not burdening either debtors or creditors in an excessive manner. During the discussion, an interesting plan for the introduction of a register of ineffective executions was mentioned. The present law does not contain this solution, but it seems that this could improve the transparency of the enforcement process in the future. On the other hand, the question of the effectiveness of the proposed funding model in the future for the full stability of the functioning of enforcement bodies in the country is an open question. The voices of the bailiffs indicate that part of the bailiffs may be closed, which will have a negative impact on economic and civil trade due to the lack of debt enforcement. On the other hand, there are examples of excessive burden on debtors leading to their total insolvency and thus also to harm creditors who will never recover their debt. First of all, it should be noted that bailiffs are designated as public officials in the draft law and act as public authorities on behalf of the State by executing judgments on behalf of the Republic of Poland. The flexibility of solutions should be taken into account in specific issues. It goes on for example. That the bailiff will only be able to grant a lawyer a minimum wage. In fact, that means half the bar rate on a given type of case. The direction of the changes should be appreciated and it should be noted that both the maximum fees and the minimum charges have been reduced in the draft law. We should particularly seek to improve the enforcement process. It must be borne in mind that the bailiff is an institution of civil law, and this is not, colloquially speaking, a person who would be involved in stripping, leading to the collapse of debtors or damage to creditors. It is a person who acts without taking into account the motivation of the debtor or the fault of the debtor, because it was already at an earlier stage of court proceedings that judged the independent court and decided so in the judgment given. Therefore, it is wrong to oppose the interest of the creditor and the debtor to the interests of the bailiff in terms of conflict. It is about ensuring the safety of the trading and efficiency of the enforcement process. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The Free and Solidarni Circle supports the government's draft bill on bail-out costs. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u76	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the club of the Polish People's Party, I have the honour to present our position on the report of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights on the Government's draft bill on bail-out costs (prints No 1581 and 2030). The aim of the proposed law is to introduce transparent rules for the financing of the judicial enforcement system and arrangements to motivate debtors to voluntarily repay the obligations identified by enforcement titles. The project also sets out the amount of the bail-in costs and the rules for carrying them, as well as the procedure for dealing with them. According to the project, the bail-in costs include the expenses of the bailiff in the course of the proceedings and the fees of the bailiffs. These will include enforcement fees and fees for other proceedings or other actions. It is essentially about enforcement procedures and other proceedings conducted by the bailiff, e.g. for the purpose of drawing up an inventory, as well as other activities commissioned by the bailiff, which are not executional activities, e.g. service of letters. This means that the traditional allocation of costs into fees and expenses has been maintained. It is maintained that the final provision on the costs due to the bailiff is enforceable without a declaration of enforceability. However, the new solution is to indicate that no enforcement fee will be charged for the execution of bail-in costs. This will eliminate situations where the provisions relating to the costs due were the basis for the initiation of separate enforcement proceedings for an additional fee and before a different bailiff than the one which issued the order. The draft law states that, as so far, the bailiff must be reimbursed in the course of the proceedings or in the course of other activities specified in the law. Therefore, a closed catalogue of expenditure incurred by the bailiff has been indicated. They'll be in it, for example. the costs of obtaining the documents or information necessary for the conduct of the proceedings and the delivery of correspondence, with the exception of, inter alia, the cost of service to the parties to the notice of initiation of the execution. The new solution is to recognise that expenditure does not constitute costs related to the transfer of funds, except for postal transfers and costs related to the transfer of funds abroad. Other expenditure related to the transfer of funds will be costs of the commercial operation and will not be charged to the parties to the proceedings. This means reducing the burden on the parties on enforcement costs. For the same purpose, the amount of the advance on the costs related to the delivery of correspondence in the case was limited to PLN 60. If expenditure for this purpose is higher, it can be deducted from the debtor. The draft law regulates the principle of the creditor's payment of advances on expenditure relating to activities outside the jurisdiction of the bailiff chosen by the creditor. The rules governing the collection by the bailiff of advances on expenditure incurred by him have also been laid down. An important novelty is the obligation of the bailiff to inform the parties of the current balance of advances in each letter addressed to them. A new solution is also the introduction of rules on the remuneration of the guardian established by the court. The project takes into account the current principle that in proceedings other than the execution of cash benefits, e.g. in cases for the execution of non-monetary benefits or for the execution of a security for the claim, the bailiff shall make the action subject to the payment of the creditor's fee. A new principle is to determine the amount of minimum, maximum and fixed charges in a quota manner, rather than in relation to the level of remuneration in the national economy, as is currently the case. There is no reasonable basis for the fees to rise with the increase in wages in the economy. The new solution will ensure the stability and predictability of the costs of execution during its duration. In order to increase the transparency of the bailiff's activities, it was proposed that he should settle the fees levied at least once a year. At the same time, the amount of enforcement fees has been rationalised by indicating that the minimum fee is PLN 100 and the maximum amount must not exceed PLN 50 000. zł. This is a significant sum and therefore the one-off fee of the bailiff should not be higher than that amount. The project also presents solutions for enforcement fees in cash benefit enforcement cases. It has been established that, as a general rule, the enforcement fee has a fixed amount of 10% the enforcement of the claim. This solution means eliminating two current rates: basic - 15% and reduced - 8%. The rates on the debtor's payments have also been amended, taking into account the principle that the amount of the fee is to depend on the actual work of the bailiff and the attitude of the debtor, which can regulate the benefit voluntarily and immediately. Therefore, if the debtor pays the amount due directly to the bailiff or to his bank account within 14 days of the date of his notification of his execution, the bailiff shall charge him a fee of 3% the value of the benefits enforced and if such payment is made after that date and the fee is 5%. A new solution was envisaged for the fee to be levied in the event of redemption of the enforcement procedure at the creditor's request or because of his inaction: in such a case, the creditor will have to pay a fee of 5%. the benefit remaining to be enforced. Currently, in such cases, the enforcement fee shall be charged to the debtor. After the changes, the debtor will bear this fee only if the creditor demonstrates that the termination of the proceedings was due to the fact that, after the initiation of the procedure, the debtor has fulfilled the benefit or has concluded an agreement with the creditor on the manner or date of its fulfilment. The principle has been introduced that, in the event of non-indebtedness, the bailiff will be able to charge a fee of 10% in the form of a non-indebted person. the benefits enforced. This should eliminate situations where creditors do not exercise due diligence when characterising the debtor, e.g. indicate the incorrect address and thus lead enforcement actions against third parties. This solution should also limit cases where the creditor refers to the bailiff an application for execution, despite the prior fulfilment of the debtor's provision. It was assumed that, in the case of a request for the execution of a money claim security and a European bank account security order, the principle that the fee is payable by the creditor at the stage of its submission would be maintained. This fee will increase from 2% to 5%. As regards other charges - for activities other than execution - a fixed fee of PLN 400 is provided, e.g. for the execution by the bailiff of the order to secure the inheritance and to draw up an inventory. To date, the inventory fee has been set for the number of hours the bailiff has worked, which has not been an incentive to carry out the proceedings properly. An important change made to the draft during the committee's work is the amendment to the article. Article 29 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 5 of the proposed law on costs, according to which the final fee will not be charged to natural persons in receipt of employee or compensation claims, to local government units, and - which is crucial - where enforcement proceedings have been initiated before the expiry of 2 years following the establishment of the enforcement title. High Izbo! We do not question the need for a separate bill on the costs of bailiffs, especially in view of the simultaneous preparation of a separate bill regulating the system and the rules governing the pursuit of the profession of bailiff. The collection and ordering of the matter covered by the project should be approved, including the consideration of issues which need to be regulated in connection with enforcement practice. The draft law on bail-out costs rationalises enforcement fees. The current profitability of bailiffs remains uneven with earnings in other legal professions, as well as with the status of bailiff as a public officer. Therefore, it is necessary to rationalise the enforcement fees that burden debtors and affect the extent to which creditors are satisfied. One element of rationalisation is e.g. preferences for those debtors who, without the use of labour-intensive coercion, fulfil their obligations without delay by paying money to the bailiff.

If I do not hear opposition, I will think that the Sejm has accepted the offer. I can't hear you objecting. I'm calling a five-minute break. ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u78	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present a report on the work of the Committee on Infrastructure on the Senate bill amending the Act on driving vehicles from print No. 1714, which is included in print No. 2244. Before I go to the committee's recommendation, I would like to stress that the very purpose of amending the law was adopted very well by the members of the committee. The abolition for disabled persons of the practical part of the driving licence test where this examination is not carried out by a vehicle of a voivodship road centre, but by a vehicle requiring adaptation to the type of disease, is a regulation expected by the environment of persons with disabilities. It is proof that the current Parliament wants to improve the working conditions of such people. The Polish state should facilitate, simplify the lives of people who have suffered serious disabilities, relieve them financially. That's what's gonna happen with this project. In the current legal state of a disabled person with a medical certificate stating that he can drive a motor vehicle after adapting it to a type of illness, where the driving of an examination is carried out by the vehicle of that person, the fee is reduced by half - from 140 PLN to 70 PLN, and after the adoption of this law disabled persons by it they will not bear such a fee at all. The Committee on Infrastructure has tabled two substantive amendments, albeit not fundamentally changing this project. These amendments concern the date of entry into force of the Act and the date of appointment by the Minister responsible for the economic affairs of the entity, who will be the entity competent to maintain and update the online database on the training centres of drivers, which they have, inter alia. vehicles adapted to the needs of persons with disabilities. Following a brief but factual discussion held in committee on 6 February, the committee unanimously requests the adoption of this draft law. From this point on, I would like to thank all the members of the Committee on Infrastructure for a genuinely substantive and substantive discussion. This project is the project that brought the High House together. Each individual's comments and voices were intended to clarify the rules and to create a legal act which would meet expectations and which would not, on the other hand, be challenged in any way by legislators or other persons who control Polish legislation. So that I thank all the Members and repeat that the committee is asking for the bill to be adopted in its entirety. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

This is the minister who, in fact, supervises matters related to the needs of people with disabilities. The Minister's supervision also includes PFRON, which finances various types of activities. And there are no obstacles to entrust this technical part to an institution that is not a participant in the training market dedicated to people with disabilities. ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u96	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! I would like to present the position of the Polish Free and Solidarni Circle in the case of the Senate's draft law amending the Act on Drivers of Vehicles. This draft adapts the Polish law to the judgment of the Constitutional Tribunal of 8 June 2016. The Court ruled that the provisions of the Act of 5 January 2011 on driving vehicles were incompatible with the Constitution of the Republic of Poland and the Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities, drawn up in New York on 13 December 2006. Disabled people who want to get a driver's license and use cars on a daily basis have significant difficulties. In the car dealership it is virtually impossible to buy a car adapted to disabled drivers. The adaptation of the vehicle is an additional cost of 10 His values. You can't make changes on your own. Only 50% of the cost of changes is reimbursed by the Disabled Drivers Association. It is therefore necessary to introduce additional reductions. Today, the practical part of the driving licence test for a disabled person is carried out by a vehicle at the disposal of a voivodship road centre. However, as contested by the Constitutional Court, Article. Article 53 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 Point 4 (l) above shall not apply, inter alia. for vehicles referred to in the case of a disabled person for which the vehicle is required to be adapted to its type of disease if the test is carried out on the vehicle of that person. A provision shall then apply providing that the practical part of the State driving licence test on application and the cost of the tested person shall be carried out by the designated driving school vehicle. For such an examination the person passing the exam must pay 140 PLN. This Act reduces payment by 50%. It should also be noted here that not all WORDs have vehicles adapted to the needs of people with disabilities. For example, the Western Pomeranian centre does not have any such vehicle, so that the passing of the test must hire such a vehicle from the training centre of drivers. The explanatory memorandum of this law rightly refers to the article. 69 of the Constitution, which provides that persons with disabilities are to be assisted by public authorities, in accordance with the Act, in safeguarding their existence, their adoption and social communication. The State should take effective measures to enable people with disabilities to have personal mobility and as much autonomy as possible. WORD should allow the disabled person to take a practical examination in a vehicle which he himself will provide, but at the same time the State should provide financial assistance to reduce the fee for carrying out the practical part of the State driving licence category B by half. In view of the above, the Free and Solidarni Circle supports the bill in the presented form.
ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u97	The project concerns the abolition for disabled persons of the practical part of the State driving licence test where the examination is not carried out by a vehicle of a voivodship road centre, but by a vehicle of a disabled person adapted to the type of illness, and the creation of an online database of information on available vehicles for the examination. The Polish People's Party Club - the European Union of Democrats will vote in favour of the bill presented in prints 1714 and 2244.

And so in the article. The 13th Act on the rules for the management of state property was corrected erroneously by the company PGE Polska Grupa Energetyczna Spółka Akcyjna (PGE Polska Grupa Energetyczna Spółka Akcyjna). Article 19 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 point (1) (a) a-j, i.e. have confirmation of the submission of the examination for candidates for members of supervisory boards. In addition, those candidates were not subject to the prohibition to remain in employment with the company and the subsidiary. The draft introduces provisions to extend employee participation in the selection of supervisory authorities of entities which are created as a result of commercialisation and which are currently operating within the structure of the group. The proposed provisions extend the powers of employees to select members of supervisory boards for employees of all subsidiaries of the company arising from commercialisation, which at the same time has the status of parent company. ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u101	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I am pleased to present my position on the draft parliamentary law amending the Law on the Rules of Government Property Management and the Commercialisation Act and certain rights of employees, prints No 2235 and 2247. Ladies and gentlemen! The aim of this project is to amend the provisions of two legal acts. The first one is a law on the rules for the management of state property. As part of the changes in the content of this law, the name of the Polish Energy Group contained in the article was improved. 13. This amendment is a correction of the error and there is no doubt about it. The second of the changes in the content of this Act is the implementation of requests made by the social side. They concern statutory requirements for representatives of employees elected to the supervisory authorities of companies of the State Treasury and public legal persons. Currently, these requirements are identical to those for candidates for members of the supervisory body indicated by the State Treasury. After the amendment, candidates selected by employees as members of supervisory boards will be exempt from the requirement to have a positive opinion of the Board of Companies with the participation of the State Treasury and public legal persons, the requirement to have a higher education or the requirement to have a confirmation of the examination for candidates for members of supervisory boards. We believe that employees should decide more freely who will represent their interests in the supervisory bodies of the companies in which they are employed. Furthermore, the selection of the appropriate persons should not be restricted by high requirements, justified in the case of representatives of the State Treasury. In our opinion, employees who care about the development of the company and the efficient functioning of the employees of their companies will be able to choose the right people to represent their interests. Further amendments concern the provisions of the Commercialisation Act and certain rights of employees. These changes aim to extend the participation of employees of commercialisation entities which are currently operating within the structure of the group. The definition of a group, a parent and a subsidiary has been introduced into a provision containing a catalogue of legal definitions. They are identical to the definitions contained in the Accounting Act. The fundamental amendment to the Commercialisation Act concerns the extension of employee rights in the selection of members of supervisory boards to employees of all subsidiaries of the company resulting from commercialisation, which is also a parent company. We believe that this increase in cultivation is intended to protect workers' interests in the changing realities of the functioning of economic operators. The current legal situation, in which the employees of the subsidiaries within the group were not able to participate in the selection of the members of the supervisory authorities of the parent company, placed them in a worse position than the employees of the company in question. This change was also advocated and supported by the social side. We, as Parliamentary Club of Law and Justice, agree to this, too. The proposal for transitional provisions contained in the draft law assumes that the companies affected by the above amendments to the Act will have 45 days from its entry into force to make appropriate amendments to their statutes and other internal acts. From this point on, they will be subject to new rules on the participation of employees of subsidiaries in the selection of members of supervisory authorities. We have also noted the need to clarify the content of the new provision contained in the article. Article 14 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 3 Commercialisation Act and some employee rights. We therefore propose an appropriate amendment which resolves any doubts as to the powers of the employees of the capital groups, including the dismissal of their representatives from the Supervisory Board. Parliamentary Club Law and Justice supports the amendment proposed by the Commission for Energy and the Treasury, supplemented by the amendment mentioned. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

In this respect, it is important to ask ourselves how this change will affect the quality of the work of the entire supervisory board and whether the person appointed by the crew will fulfil its tasks in controlling the work of the management board, because that is the power of the supervisory board, but also whether it will participate in the process of developing a strategy for the development of companies in one group. The justification for the bill says that we will appoint a crew member to the Supervisory Board, based only on his general knowledge of the company and the trust of the employees themselves, and that, in our opinion, is far too, far too little to be entrusted to such a person as a member of the Supervisory Board. Besides, I don't think such a person will be able to assess the budget of such a company because she doesn't know it, she doesn't even know what the budget is, she can't even talk about supervising the work of the board. Therefore, this is some member who does not know what function he will perform. The state before me said that this person would represent the crew in terms of workers' rights. Ladies and gentlemen, this is the role of a member of the team appointed by the trade unions, it does not have to be a member of the supervisory board. A member of the supervisory board assumes full responsibility for the operation of the company, for the quality of its work, for the quality of the application of the law, for the fact that this company will develop, that it will have plans to approve. How is he supposed to approve plans if he doesn't know these plans? He may know about the employment relationship between the employee and the employer. But it can't be on the principle that this member will judge the work of the board. Ladies and gentlemen, this should not have happened and we should not have introduced such procedures. Therefore, the Civic Platform cannot support the proposed amendments and believes that this law must be put in the basket as soon as possible. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2018-02-27-sejm-59-1.u113	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Polish Free and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the report of the committee on the draft law amending the Law on the Rules for the Management of State Property and the Commercialisation Act and certain rights of employees. According to the reasons given, the proposed law amends the requirements to be met by persons designated as a member of the supervisory authority by an entity entitled to exercise rights from shares belonging to the Treasury or a State legal person, including persons selected to the supervisory authority by employees of companies with the State Treasury or public legal persons, and the provisions concerning the Board of Companies with the participation of the State Treasury and public legal persons. Persons selected for the supervisory authority by employees will not have to meet the requirements so far necessary to become a member of the supervisory board. In addition, those candidates were not subject to the prohibition to remain in employment with the company and the subsidiary. The proposal also introduces provisions to extend the participation of employees in the selection of supervisory authorities of entities which are created as a result of commercialisation and which are currently operating within the structure of the group. The proposed provisions extend the powers of staff to select members for supervisory boards to employees of all subsidiaries of the company resulting from commercialisation, which at the same time has the status of parent company. The aim of these changes is to extend the rights of workers, taking into account the structure and links of entities operating within one group and thereby increasing the participation of workers within the group. These representatives are elected by the crew in the general election. Their task is above all to defend the interests of the crew and not to allow the conditions established during the commercialisation of the company to deteriorate. These conditions concerning collective agreements, remuneration and other elements were a necessary condition for the consent of the social party to commercialisation. The most important thing in this case is trust in these people on the social side, not their formal competence. It is completely unfounded to claim that these persons may be detrimental to the company as a result of incompetence. It is in the interests of the crew's representatives, apart from the permanent pursuit of their interests, as well as the interests of the company, to take care of this entity's well-being. If the company itself falls, then neither will the crew be needed. It's a logical relationship. Employees identify with their company, with their place of work and in their own interest themselves will ensure that their representative is a person of high competence, although not necessarily confirmed by documents. It is in their interest that it is not a figurine to raise hands, but a person who knows the specifics of the industry and, above all, who has experience in working in this particular company, in this particular workplace, which she is an employee of. This is a bow towards the trade union organisations of the various companies and the trade union centres which have called for it. The obligation to carry out the duties set out in the Act, their scope, internal supervision, and therefore very, very broadly, is to refer to the subjectivity, so who is subject to it, and to the rules that are to apply. With regard to government administration, the project shall also institutionally define who and to what extent it carries out the tasks resulting from the project. The bodies of financial information on anti-money laundering and terrorist financing will be, whether it is decided that it will be, the Minister responsible for public finances and the General Inspector for Financial Information. The Financial Security Committee will have an opinion and consultative role in this respect. The General Inspector will determine the national risk assessment for money laundering and terrorist financing and then develop a strategy based on this risk assessment, an action plan to reduce this risk. All institutions are obliged to apply financial security measures to their clients to a very, very large extent. The central part of money laundering and terrorist financing, particularly decision-making, is the responsibility of the General Financial Information Inspector, whose competences and responsibilities are also very, very precise in this project. Following a thorough analysis of the government project, the Committee on Public Finance submits to the High House to adopt a proposal with minor substantive changes and a slightly larger number of legislative changes, which were highlighted by the Legislative Office of the Sejm. In the course of the discussion, a number of comments were submitted on behalf of the institutions required for the provisions of the draft law which went beyond the scope of the Directive. Some of them, although to a very small extent, have been taken into account, but most of them, unfortunately, were rejected by the majority of the Public Finance Commission when the government accepted the party. Mrs. Marshal! On behalf of the Polish People's Party Club, I will make two amendments. One of these concerns the deletion in Article. 11 point 3, which assumes that the general inspector may be a person who presents an impeccable moral, civic and patriotic attitude. This record is generally considered to be such an objective overzealousness. None of the terms used, whether moral, civic or patriotic, can relate to an objective assessment, and each of them will always depend on who, when and on what he will issue this assessment.
